Findet neue Duplikate (dieselbe Regel in mehreren Repos), Widersprüche (dieselbe Regel sagt an zwei Orten Verschiedenes), tote Zeiger, fest verdrahtete Branch-Namen und Anweisungen, die ins Leere greifen. - **auroc-evaluation-across-cell-types**: Use when benchmarking a sequence model on the DNALONGBENCH eQTL task and you need per-cell-type AUROC values plus an average to compare against the paper's reported scores (Expert Model average AUROC 0.681). - **auroc-metric-evaluation-of-gene-detection**: Use when benchmarking cluster-free DE methods on single-cell data with known ground-truth DE labels (e.g., the simulated COVID-19 cohort 1 dataset) and you need per-method AUROC of up- and down-regulated gene detection alongside Pearson r. - **auroc-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained sequence classifier (e.g., a CNN scoring candidate polyA sites) must be benchmarked on held-out genomic test sets from independent databases, with AUROC as the accuracy metric. - **auroc-performance-comparison-across-datasets**: Use when you need to test whether a motif-count feature category (e.g., overlapping vs. non-overlapping FIMO annotations) is biologically informative by retraining BagOfMotifs models and comparing auROC before and after the ablation. - **auroc-performance-computation**: Use when comparing novel cell type detection or cell-type classification methods on single-cell RNA-seq query/reference pairs and you need threshold-independent performance metrics such as AUROC, AUPR and FPR95 per method per setting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/auroc-performance-computation))
